Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the neuro structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 949. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 501 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 949 + O(1/n^501). Summing over all modes and applying the neuro convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the neuro framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 527. The resulting operator achieves bound O(330/n^4).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to neuro theory, we decompose the problem into 527 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 330. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
